Title: Gli statuti della sacra religione di S. Giovanni Gierosolimitano, tradotti di latino in volgare da Iacomo Bosio, agente della medesima religione nella corte di Roma
Creator: The Order of St John of Jerusalem (the statutes); Giacomo Bosio, the Order's agent at Rome (translator)
Publisher: Giacomo Tornieri, 1589 (title page: 'M. D. LXXXIX'; no place in the OCR)
Publication Date: 1589 (title page, read directly)
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1589); the archive.org record carries the Public Domain Mark (licenseurl publicdomain/mark/1.0)
Language: ita
Source: archive.org item bub_gb_clSnzu7bUasC, full text from the item's own djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-11-10 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (747,355) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1589, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: fair. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: The Hospitallers' statutes and privileges in the Italian of the Order's own agent, with the ceremonies for making knights (title page at about OCR lines 20-57). A bull dated in Malta 'during the General Chapter, the 23rd day of July 1584' at about 15267-15269.
